country_event = {
	id = dtp.30000
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		from = {
			dxxohrp = yes
			dxgtkrc = yes
			dxdo31j = yes
			set_variable = { which = dtp_rr value = -1 }
			set_variable = { which = dtp_rk value = 0 }
		}
		dtp_load = yes
		dtp_sp_load = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = event_target:dtp_t
			}
			set_country_flag = dtp_ok_tc
		}
		else = {
			remove_country_flag = dtp_ok_tc
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = event_target:dtp_r
			}
			set_country_flag = dtp_ok_rcp
		}
		else = {
			remove_country_flag = dtp_ok_rcp
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = event_target:dtp_p
			}
			set_country_flag = dtp_ok_tp
		}
		else = {
			remove_country_flag = dtp_ok_tp
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = event_target:dtp_f
			}
			set_country_flag = dtp_ok_tf
		}
		else = {
			remove_country_flag = dtp_ok_tf
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_dis value = 1 }
				}
			}
			dtp_disarm = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 75 }
				}
			}
			from = {
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rc0 value = 7 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rc value = 7 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rk value = 1 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 77 }
				}
			}
			from = {
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rc0 value = 32 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rc value = 32 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rk value = 2 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_act value >= 80 }
					check_variable = { which = dtp_act value <= 81 }
				}
			}
			from = {
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rc0 value = 36 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rc value = 36 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rk value = 2 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_act value >= 84 }
					check_variable = { which = dtp_act value <= 87 }
				}
			}
			from = {
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rc0 value = 36 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rc value = 36 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rk value = 2 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 89 }
				}
			}
			from = {
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rc0 value = 32 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rc value = 32 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_rk value = 2 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_rk value > 0 }
				}
			}
			from = {
				set_variable = { which = dtp_ok value = 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					from = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_rk value = 1 }
						dxcyym2 = { P = planet }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = event_target:dtp_r
					}
					from = {
						set_variable = { which = dtp_ok value = 1 }
					}
				}
				else = {
					from = {
						set_variable = { which = dtp_ok value = 2 }
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					from = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_rk value = 2 }
						dxcyym2 = { P = planet }
					}
				}
				from = {
					set_variable = { which = dtp_ok value = 1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					from = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_ok value = 0 }
					}
				}
				from = {
					set_variable = { which = dtp_rr value = 0 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					from = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_ok value = 2 }
					}
				}
				from = {
					set_variable = { which = dtp_rr value = 2 }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_rk value > 0 }
					check_variable = { which = dtp_ok value = 1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					from = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 75 }
					}
				}
				every_system = {
					limit = {
						has_star_flag = dtp_marked
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							exists = starbase
						}
						starbase = {
							set_owner = event_target:dtp_r
						}
					}
					else = {
						create_starbase = {
							owner = event_target:dtp_r
							size = starbase_outpost
						}
					}
					every_system_planet = {
						limit = {
							exists = owner
						}
						set_owner = event_target:dtp_r
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_colony = yes
							}
							set_controller = event_target:dtp_r
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					from = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 77 }
					}
				}
				every_system = {
					limit = {
						has_star_flag = dtp_marked
					}
					log = "DTP FREE system"
					every_system_planet = {
						limit = {
							exists = orbital_station
						}
						orbital_station = {
							dismantle = yes
						}
					}
					every_starbase_in_system = {
						ship = {
							destroy_fleet = this
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					from = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 80 }
					}
				}
				every_system = {
					from = {
						set_visited = prev
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					from = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 81 }
					}
				}
				from = {
					every_system = {
						every_system_planet = {
							set_surveyed = {
								surveyed = yes
								surveyor = prevprev
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					from = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 84 }
					}
				}
				from = {
					save_event_target_as = dtp_mk_own
					capital_scope = {
						save_event_target_as = dtp_mk_cap
					}
					create_fleet = {
						name = "dtp_marker_fleet"
						effect = {
							set_owner = event_target:dtp_mk_own
							create_ship = {
								name = "dtp_marker_ship"
								design = "DTP_Marker"
								graphical_culture = owner
							}
							set_location = {
								target = event_target:dtp_mk_cap
								distance = 20
								angle = random
							}
							set_fleet_flag = dtp_marker
							set_fleet_stance = passive
							set_cloaking_active = yes
							every_owned_ship = {
								add_modifier = {
									modifier = dtp_marker_ship
									days = -1
								}
							}
							fleet_event = {
								id = dtp.1281
								days = 1
							}
						}
					}
					log = "DTP MARKER: ship created"
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					from = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 85 }
					}
				}
				from = {
					every_owned_fleet = {
						limit = {
							has_fleet_flag = dtp_marker
						}
						destroy_fleet = this
					}
					log = "DTP MARKER: ship removed"
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					from = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 86 }
					}
				}
				from = {
					every_owned_fleet = {
						limit = {
							has_fleet_flag = dtp_marker
						}
						set_timed_fleet_flag = {
							flag = dtp_mk_tp
							days = 1
						}
						set_location = {
							target = event_target:dtp_cur
							distance = 25
							angle = random
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					from = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 87 }
					}
				}
				from = {
					capital_scope = {
						save_event_target_as = dtp_mk_cap
					}
					every_owned_fleet = {
						limit = {
							has_fleet_flag = dtp_marker
						}
						set_timed_fleet_flag = {
							flag = dtp_mk_tp
							days = 1
						}
						set_location = {
							target = event_target:dtp_mk_cap
							distance = 20
							angle = random
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					from = {
						check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 89 }
					}
				}
				every_system = {
					limit = {
						has_star_flag = dtp_marked

								AND = {
									check_variable = {
										which = dtp_in
										value = 0
									}
									has_country_flag = dtp_kneg
								}
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = {
							which = dtp_in
							value = 10
						}
						change_variable = {
							which = dtp_in
							value = 0
						}
					}
					else = {
						multiply_variable = {
							which = dtp_in
							value = 10
						}
						change_variable = {
							which = dtp_in
							value = 0
						}
					}

						limit = {
							check_variable = {
								which = dtp_in
								value > 999999999
							}
						}
						set_variable = {
							which = dtp_in
							value = 999999999
						}
					}

								which = dtp_in
								value < -999999999
							}
						}
						set_variable = {
							which = dtp_in
							value = -999999999
						}

						value = dtp_in
					}

						which = dtp_amount
						value = dtp_in
					}

				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_2 value = dtp_logcode_1 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_2 value = dtp_logres_1 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log1_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_tgt
			}
			from = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_1 value = dtp_rc }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_1 value = dtp_rr }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_last_code value = dtp_rc }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_last_ok value = dtp_rr }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_t
				}
				event_target:dtp_t = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			else = {
				from = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			log = "DTP | code [From.dtp_rc] | result [From.dtp_rr]"
		}
	}
}

				}
			}
			log = "DTP | code [From.dtp_rc] | result [From.dtp_rr]"
		}
	}

			event_target:dtp_log1_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_tgt
			}
			from = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_1 value = dtp_rc }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_1 value = dtp_rr }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_last_code value = dtp_rc }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_last_ok value = dtp_rr }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_t
				}
				event_target:dtp_t = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			else = {
				from = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			log = "DTP | code [From.dtp_rc] | result [From.dtp_rr]"
		}
	}
}

							which = dtp_ry
							value = 500
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 398 }
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				from = {
					multiply_variable = {
						which = dtp_ry
						value = 10
					}
					change_variable = {
						which = dtp_ry
						value = 5
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = {
								which = dtp_ry
								value > 500
							}
						}
						set_variable = {
							which = dtp_ry
							value = 500
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 399 }
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				from = {
					multiply_variable = {
						which = dtp_ry
						value = 10
					}
					change_variable = {
						which = dtp_ry
						value = 6
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = {
								which = dtp_ry
								value > 500
							}
						}
						set_variable = {
							which = dtp_ry
							value = 500
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_act value = 400 }
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				from = {
					multiply_variable = {
						which = dtp_ry
						value = 10
					}
					change_variable = {
						which = dtp_ry
						value = 7
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = {
								which = dtp_ry
								value > 500
							}
						}
						set_variable = {
							which = dtp_ry
							value = 500
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		from = {
			dtp_vsync = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				from = {
					check_variable = { which = dtp_rr value > -1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { exists = event_target:dtp_log8_actor }
				}
				from = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_1 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_1 value = 0 }
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_tgt
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_2 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_2 value = 0 }
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log3_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log3_tgt
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_3 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_3 value = 0 }
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log4_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log4_tgt
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_4 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_4 value = 0 }
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log5_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log5_tgt
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_5 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_5 value = 0 }
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log6_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log6_tgt
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_6 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_6 value = 0 }
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log7_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log7_tgt
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_7 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_7 value = 0 }
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log8_actor
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log8_tgt
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_8 value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_8 value = 0 }
				}
			}
			event_target:dtp_log7_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log8_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_8 value = dtp_logcode_7 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_8 value = dtp_logres_7 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log7_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log8_tgt
			}
			event_target:dtp_log6_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log7_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_7 value = dtp_logcode_6 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_7 value = dtp_logres_6 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log6_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log7_tgt
			}
			event_target:dtp_log5_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log6_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_6 value = dtp_logcode_5 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_6 value = dtp_logres_5 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log5_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log6_tgt
			}
			event_target:dtp_log4_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log5_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_5 value = dtp_logcode_4 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_5 value = dtp_logres_4 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log4_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log5_tgt
			}
			event_target:dtp_log3_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log4_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_4 value = dtp_logcode_3 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_4 value = dtp_logres_3 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log3_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log4_tgt
			}
			event_target:dtp_log2_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log3_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_3 value = dtp_logcode_2 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_3 value = dtp_logres_2 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log2_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log3_tgt
			}
			event_target:dtp_log1_actor = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_2 value = dtp_logcode_1 }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_2 value = dtp_logres_1 }
			}
			event_target:dtp_log1_tgt = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log2_tgt
			}
			from = {
				save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_actor
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logcode_1 value = dtp_rc }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_logres_1 value = dtp_rr }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_last_code value = dtp_rc }
				set_variable = { which = dtp_last_ok value = dtp_rr }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:dtp_t
				}
				event_target:dtp_t = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			else = {
				from = {
					save_global_event_target_as = dtp_log1_tgt
				}
			}
			log = "DTP | code [From.dtp_rc] | result [From.dtp_rr]"
		}
	}
}
